Run DiD regressions\n        for feature in feature_cols:\n            try:\n                if feature not in cat_intensive.columns:\n                    continue\n\n                # Check for variation\n                feat_var = cat_intensive[feature].var()\n                if feat_var == 0 or pd.isna(feat_var) or feat_var < 0.001:\n                    continue\n\n                # Check if feature has both 0 and 1 values\n                unique_vals = cat_intensive[feature].unique()\n                if len(unique_vals) < 2:\n                    continue\n\n                # Create interaction term\n                cat_intensive['interaction'] = cat_intensive[feature] * cat_intensive['post']\n\n                # Run OLS regression\n                formula = f'Total_Spend ~ {feature} + post + interaction'\n                model = smf.ols(formula, data=cat_intensive).fit()\n\n                if 'interaction' in model.params:\n                    intensive_results.append({\n                        'feature': feature,\n                        'did_estimate': float(model.params['interaction']),\n                        'p_value': float(model.pvalues['interaction'])\n                    })\n            except Exception as e:\n                continue\n\n    # Run DiD regressions\n        for feature in feature_cols:\n            try:\n                if feature not in cat_extensive.columns:\n                    continue\n\n                # Check for variation\n                feat_var = cat_extensive[feature].var()\n                if feat_var == 0 or pd.isna(feat_var) or feat_var < 0.001:\n                    continue\n\n                # Linear probability model\n                formula = f'Has_Purchase ~ {feature} + post + interaction'\n                model = smf.ols(formula, data=cat_extensive).fit()\n\n                if 'interaction' in model.params:\n                    extensive_results.append({\n                        'feature': feature,\n                        'did_estimate': float(model.params['interaction']),\n                        'p_value': float(model.pvalues['interaction'])\n                    })\n            except Exception as e:\n                continue\n\n    # Run DiD regressions\n        for feature in feature_cols:\n            try:\n                if feature not in cat_intensive.columns:\n                    continue\n\n                # Check for variation\n                feat_var = cat_intensive[feature].var()\n                if feat_var == 0 or pd.isna(feat_var) or feat_var < 0.001:\n                    continue\n\n                # Check if feature has both 0 and 1 values\n                unique_vals = cat_intensive[feature].unique()\n                if len(unique_vals) < 2:\n                    continue\n\n                # Create interaction term\n                cat_intensive['interaction'] = cat_intensive[feature] * cat_intensive['post']\n\n                # Get anomaly index\n    anomaly_index = anomaly_df[anomaly_df['Category'] == category]['Anomaly_Index'].values\n    if len(anomaly_index) == 0:\n        return None\n    anomaly_index = float(anomaly_index[0])\n\n    # ========================\n    # INTENSIVE MARGIN ANALYSIS\n    # ========================\n    intensive_results = []\n    cat_intensive = intensive_data[intensive_data['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n    baseline_spend = 0\n    treatment_spend = 0\n    n_baseline = 0\n    n_treatment = 0\n\n    if len(cat_intensive) >= 30:  # Minimum sample for regression\n        # Merge features\n        cat_intensive = cat_intensive.merge(df_features, on='Survey ResponseID', how='left')\n\n        # Fill missing with 0\n        for col in feature_cols:\n            if col in cat_intensive.columns:\n                cat_intensive[col] = pd.to_numeric(cat_intensive[col], errors='coerce').fillna(0)\n\n        # Period indicator\n        cat_intensive['post'] = (cat_intensive['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n        # Stats\n        baseline_spend = cat_intensive[cat_intensive['Period'] == 'baseline']['Total_Spend'].mean()\n        treatment_spend = cat_intensive[cat_intensive['Period'] == 'treatment']['Total_Spend'].mean()\n        n_baseline = len(cat_intensive[cat_intensive['Period'] == 'baseline']['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n        n_treatment = len(cat_intensive[cat_intensive['Period'] == 'treatment']['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n\n        # Run DiD regressions\n        for feature in feature_cols:\n            try:\n                if feature not in cat_intensive.columns:\n                    continue\n\n                # Check for variation\n                feat_var = cat_intensive[feature].var()\n                if feat_var == 0 or pd.isna(feat_var) or feat_var < 0.001:\n                    continue\n\n                # Check if feature has both 0 and 1 values\n                unique_vals = cat_intensive[feature].unique()\n                if len(unique_vals) < 2:\n                    continue\n\n                # Create interaction term\n                cat_intensive['interaction'] = cat_intensive[feature] * cat_intensive['post']\n\n                # Run OLS regression (use Q() to escape special characters)\n                formula = f'Total_Spend ~ Q(\\'{feature}\\') + post + interaction'\n                model = smf.ols(formula, data=cat_intensive).fit()\n\n                if 'interaction' in model.params:\n                    intensive_results.append({\n                        'feature': feature,\n                        'did_estimate': float(model.params['interaction']),\n                        'p_value': float(model.pvalues['interaction'])\n                    })\n            except Exception as e:\n                continue\n\n    # Sort results\n    if category_type == 'surge':\n        intensive_results.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n    else:\n        intensive_results.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'])\n\n    # Take top 3\n    intensive_top3 = intensive_results[:3]\n    for r in intensive_top3:\n        r['method'] = 'Univariate DiD'\n        r['did_estimate'] = round(r['did_estimate'], 2)\n        r['p_value'] = round(r['p_value'], 3)\n\n    # ========================\n    # EXTENSIVE MARGIN ANALYSIS\n    # ========================\n    extensive_results = []\n    cat_extensive = extensive_data[extensive_data['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n    baseline_rate = 0\n    treatment_rate = 0\n    n_at_risk = 0\n\n    if len(cat_extensive) > 0:\n        # Merge features\n        cat_extensive = cat_extensive.merge(df_features, on='Survey ResponseID', how='left')\n\n        # Fill missing with 0\n        for col in feature_cols:\n            if col in cat_extensive.columns:\n                cat_extensive[col] = pd.to_numeric(cat_extensive[col], errors='coerce').fillna(0)\n\n        # Period indicator\n        cat_extensive['post'] = (cat_extensive['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n        # Stats\n        baseline_rate = cat_extensive[cat_extensive['Period'] == 'baseline']['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n        treatment_rate = cat_extensive[cat_extensive['Period'] == 'treatment']['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n        n_at_risk = len(cat_extensive['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n\n        # Run DiD regressions\n        for feature in feature_cols:\n            try:\n                if feature not in cat_extensive.columns:\n                    continue\n\n                # Check for variation\n                feat_var = cat_extensive[feature].var()\n                if feat_var == 0 or pd.isna(feat_var) or feat_var < 0.001:\n                    continue\n\n                # Check if feature has both 0 and 1 values\n                unique_vals = cat_extensive[feature].unique()\n                if len(unique_vals) < 2:\n                    continue\n\n                # Create interaction term\n                cat_extensive['interaction'] = cat_extensive[feature] * cat_extensive['post']\n\n                # Linear probability model\n                formula = f'Has_Purchase ~ {feature} + post + interaction'\n                model = smf.ols(formula, data=cat_extensive).fit()\n\n                if 'interaction' in model.params:\n                    extensive_results.append({\n                        'feature': feature,\n                        'did_estimate': float(model.params['interaction']),\n                        'p_value': float(model.pvalues['interaction'])\n                    })\n            except Exception as e:\n                continue\n\n    # Sort results\n    if category_type == 'surge':\n        extensive_results.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n    else:\n        extensive_results.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'])\n\n    # Take top 3\n    extensive_top3 = extensive_results[:3]\n    for r in extensive_top3:\n        r['method'] = 'Multivariate Heterogeneous DiD'\n        r['did_estimate'] = round(r['did_estimate'], 4)\n        r['p_value'] = round(r['p_value'], 3)\n\n    print(f\"  Intensive: {len(intensive_top3)}/{len(intensive_results)} | Extensive: {len(extensive_top3)}/{len(extensive_results)}\")\n\n    return {\n        'category': category,\n        'anomaly_index': round(anomaly_index, 2),\n        'baseline_avg_spend': round(baseline_spend, 2),\n        'treatment_avg_spend': round(treatment_spend, 2),\n        'n_purchasers_baseline': int(n_baseline),\n        'n_purchasers_treatment': int(n_treatment),\n        'baseline_purchase_rate': round(baseline_rate, 2),\n        'treatment_purchase_rate': round(treatment_rate, 2),\n        'n_at_risk': int(n_at_risk),\n        'intensive_margin': intensive_top3,\n        'extensive_margin': extensive_top3\n    }\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    run_final_did_analysis()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":230,"oldLines":8,"newStart":230,"newLines":8,"lines":["                 #
